# Game rules

Every rule the planner relies on, in our own words, and the game code or data it was checked against.
Checked against Stardew Valley 1.6.15.24356. `scripts/rules.py check` flags any rule whose game code changes in an update.

## Calendar & events & access

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cal.season-28-days` | The day counter rolls to day 1 of the next season once it passes 28; WorldDate defines 28 days per season, 4 seasons, 112 days a year, so day-of-year = season_index*28 + day. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.year-112-days` | WorldDate.DaysPerYear is 112 (4 x 28). |  | verified |
| `cal.bus-vault` | The bus ticket counter and the Desert both key off the host having the mail flag ccVault. Completing the Vault room sends that flag (overnight), but so does buying the bus repair from the Joja Community Development form. | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.BusStop.answerDialogue`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.BusStop.checkAction`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.doAreaCompleteReward` | to fix |
| `cal.secret-woods-steel-axe` | The hollow log (resource clump 602) refuses any axe below upgrade level 2 (Steel); non-axe tools and bombs cannot remove it. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.ResourceClump.performToolAction` | verified |
| `cal.mines-landslide` | Mountain.DayUpdate clears the landslide and sends landslideDone (to everyone) once DaysPlayed >= 5, i.e. on the morning of Spring 5 Year 1. DaysPlayed is incremented before location DayUpdate runs. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Mountain.DayUpdate` | verified |
| `cal.island-boat` | The boat tunnel lets you travel only once the host has mail willyBoatFixed (added for tomorrow, no letter, by the boat-fixed world change event). Each trip still costs the ticket. | `StardewValley.Locations.BoatTunnel.answerDialogue`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.BoatTunnel.checkAction` | verified |
| `cal.festival-names` | Festival names and dates come from Data/Festivals/FestivalDates (8 festivals, each file giving its map and hours as '<Location>/<start> <end>') and Data/PassiveFestivals (Night Market, Desert Festival with its desert condition; the Trout Derby and SquidFest are added to the calendar by the calendar's own code). | `StardewValley.Menus.Billboard.GetEventsForDay`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.isFestivalDay/4`<br>`Data/Festivals/FestivalDates + Data/Festivals/<season><day>.conditions`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `cal.year-length` | 112-day year; absolute day 1 is Spring 1 Y1 (Game1.stats.DaysPlayed is 1 on that day). | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.season-length` | 28-day seasons in order spring, summer, fall, winter; abs_day formula matches WorldDate.TotalDays+1 when no days are skipped. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.season-end-day` | Seasonal content ends with day 28 of its last season, so (last_index+1)*28 is the last day-of-year. Caveat: it is planner arithmetic; it ignores grow time for crops and season sets that would wrap winter->spring (none in data). | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.cart-days-node` | Forest cart visits when dayOfMonth % 7 % 5 == 0 (days 5,7,12,14,19,21,26,28 = Fridays and Sundays). The 'Traveler' shop is also opened by the Desert Festival and the Night Market boat. The Desert Festival only runs when LOCATION_ACCESSIBLE Desert is true. | `StardewValley.Locations.Forest.ShouldTravelingMerchantVisitToday`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)`<br>`Data/Shops Traveler.Owners` | verified |
| `cal.weekday-gate` | Desert Trader items are gated with DAY_OF_WEEK conditions (Cloth on Wednesday, Cheese on Friday) and Sandy's Friday item is Honey, so the weekday rule is right. | `Data/Shops DesertTrade.Items[].Condition, DesertTrade.Owners`<br>`Data/Shops Sandy.Items[].Condition` | to fix |
| `cal.junimo-text-trigger` | Event 611439 (Town) needs DaysPlayed > 4 (Spring 5+), time 8:00-13:00, weather not raining ('sunny' = not raining here), host only, and the farmer arriving on tile (0,54) - the west edge of town coming from the Bus Stop. Reading a bundle scroll in the CC adds seenJunimoNote and queues mail wizardJunimoNote for tomorrow; the Wizard's event 112 needs seenJunimoNote and happens on entering his tower, which opens once wizardJunimoNote is received. | `StardewValley.Locations.Forest.isWizardHouseUnlocked`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.setUpMenu`<br>`StardewValley.Preconditions.DaysPlayed`<br>`StardewValley.Preconditions.Weather`<br>`Data/Events/Town (611439)`<br>`Data/Events/WizardHouse (112)` | to fix |
| `cal.egg-festival-stall` | Festival_EggFestival_Pierre stocks (BC)36 Lawn Flamingo, (BC)107 Plush Bunny, (O)745 Strawberry Seeds 100g, (BC)184 and (BC)188 'Seasonal Plant' at 350g, and furniture. The 350g '188' is the Big Craftable Seasonal Plant, not Object 188 Green Bean. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`Data/Shops Festival_EggFestival_Pierre.Items[].ItemId/Price` | to fix |
| `cal.flower-dance-stall` | Festival_FlowerDance_Pierre sells (O)18 Daffodil 50g and (O)22 Dandelion 50g (correct), but the 350g '192' is (BC)192 Seasonal Plant, not Object 192 Potato. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`Data/Shops Festival_FlowerDance_Pierre.Items[]` | to fix |
| `cal.night-market-boat` | Festival_NightMarket_DecorationBoat sells (BC)188 and (BC)192 Seasonal Plants at 500g (Big Craftables), not Green Bean or Potato. It also has a closed owner before 17:00. | `Data/Shops Festival_NightMarket_DecorationBoat` | to fix |
| `cal.winter-star-stall` | Festival_FeastOfTheWinterStar_Pierre has three random slots, each picking one of four items with the day-save seeded random: 2,500g one of {Triple Shot Espresso, Powdermelon, Garlic, Fire Quartz}; 2,500g one of {Frozen Tear, Fried Mushroom, Duck Egg, Bread}; 500g one of {Cave Carrot, Perch, Clam, Winter Root}. One of each per player. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`StardewValley.Internal.ItemQueryResolver.TryResolve`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.CreateDaySaveRandom`<br>`Data/Shops Festival_FeastOfTheWinterStar_Pierre.Items[].RandomItemId/Price` | to fix |
| `cal.ice-festival-merchant` | Festival_FestivalOfIce_TravelingMerchant's 5,000g item is (BC)136 Rarecrow, not (O)136 Largemouth Bass. The shop has no fish. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`Data/Shops Festival_FestivalOfIce_TravelingMerchant` | to fix |
| `cal.desert-trader` | DesertTrade: (O)428 Cloth for 3 (O)62 Aquamarine with DAY_OF_WEEK Wednesday; (O)424 Cheese for 1 (O)60 Emerald with DAY_OF_WEEK Friday. | `Data/Shops DesertTrade.Items[].TradeItemId/TradeItemAmount/Condition`<br>`Data/Shops DesertTrade.Owners` | to fix |
| `cal.red-cabbage-sources-route` | The cart's random object stock can roll Red Cabbage or its seeds; the 'Traveler' shop is also at the Night Market (Winter 15-17, after 17:00) and the Desert Festival (after 12:00). In Year 1 a Red Cabbage Seeds (O)485 entry is guaranteed on the visit where VisitsUntilY1Guarantee hits 0 (counts down on cart days and Night Market days). Pierre stocks (O)485 from Year 2 in summer. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.ShouldTravelingMerchantVisitToday`<br>`Data/Shops SeedShop (O)485 'SEASON summer, YEAR 2'`<br>`Data/Shops Traveler.Items ((O)485 WORLD_STATE_FIELD VisitsUntilY1Guarantee 0 0)` | to fix |
| `cal.blackberry-bushes` | Normal berry bushes bloom in fall when day > 7 and < 12 (Fall 8-11); spring bloom is days 15-18 (salmonberry). Uses the bush location's season. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.inBloom` | verified |
| `cal.season-28-days.2` | Day 28 is the last day of every season. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.festival-gold-sinks` | Active festivals sit on fixed season/day pairs (Data/Festivals/FestivalDates) and their shops have fixed prices (e.g. Strawberry Seeds 100g). The quantity to buy (suggested_qty) is planner policy, not a game rule. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`Data/Festivals/FestivalDates + Data/Festivals/<season><day>.conditions`<br>`Data/Shops` | verified |
| `cal.queen-of-sauce-schedule` | The Sunday Queen of Sauce episode is (DaysPlayed % 224) / 7, with a remainder of 0 meaning 32: the 32 episodes air on Year 1-2 Sundays and the same cycle restarts every two years (Year 3 Spring 7 is episode 1 again), so a Year-N Sunday matches Year ((N-1) mod 2)+1. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getRerunWeek`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `cal.night-market-winter-15-17` | NightMarket: Winter 15-17, StartTime 1700, no condition. | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `cal.egg-festival-spring13` | Egg Festival is spring13; its shop sells (O)745 Strawberry Seeds at 100g. Strawberry is spring-only, 8 days to grow (1,1,2,2,2) and regrows every 4 days, so seeds planted Spring 14 give Spring 22 and 26 harvests. | `StardewValley.Event.checkAction`<br>`Data/Crops 745 (DaysInPhase, RegrowDays, Seasons)`<br>`Data/Festivals/FestivalDates + Data/Festivals/<season><day>.conditions`<br>`Data/Shops Festival_EggFestival_Pierre (O)745 Price` | verified |
| `cart.days-fri-sun` | dayOfMonth % 7 % 5 == 0 is true exactly on days that are Fridays (5,12,19,26) and Sundays (7,14,21,28). | `StardewValley.Game1.shortDayNameFromDayOfSeason`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.ShouldTravelingMerchantVisitToday` | verified |
| `qos.maki-roll-summer21` | CookingChannel episode 7 is Maki Roll -> Sunday Summer 21 Year 1 (DaysPlayed 49). Recipe needs any Fish (-4) x1, Seaweed (152) x1, Rice (423) x1. It can also be learned on a Wednesday rerun or when the cycle repeats. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/CookingRecipes 'Maki Roll'`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel '7'` | verified |
| `access.desert-vault` | isLocationAccessible('Desert') is true only when the host's mailReceived contains ccVault. The planner also accepts 'Vault room complete'. | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.doAreaCompleteReward` | to fix |
| `access.railroad-day31` | isLocationAccessible('Railroad') needs DaysPlayed > 31 (Summer 4 Y1 onward); NPC schedules and trains use this. But Mountain.DayUpdate unblocks the path when DaysPlayed >= 31, so the player can walk in on Summer 3. | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Mountain.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Railroad.DayUpdate` | verified |
| `access.cc-event-191393` | isLocationAccessible('CommunityCenter') checks the current player's eventsSeen for 191393 (the CC restoration ceremony). | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.changeScheduleForLocationAccessibility` | verified |
| `access.jojamart-until-cc` | isLocationAccessible('JojaMart') is true while no player has seen event 191393 (Utility.HasAnyPlayerSeenEvent). On the Joja route it stays open. | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible` | verified |
| `access.secret-woods-steel-axe` | Hollow log 602 needs Axe upgrade level >= 2. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.ResourceClump.performToolAction` | verified |
| `access.sewer-rusty-key` | The Forest sewer grate warps to the Sewer if mail OpenedSewer is set; otherwise, with hasRustyKey, it opens and sets that mail; else it's locked. | `StardewValley.Event.DefaultCommands.RustyKey`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.checkAction` | verified |
| `access.island-boat` | Boat travel needs host mail willyBoatFixed. |  | verified |
| `access.mines-landslide` | Landslide cleared and landslideDone sent on DaysPlayed >= 5. | `StardewValley.Locations.Mountain.DayUpdate` | verified |
| `access.mines-floor` | Treating floor N as reachable when deepestMineLevel >= N is a safe lower bound. The elevator offers floors in steps of 5 up to the deepest reached, and the player can always walk down from there. |  | verified |
| `access.desert-festival-needs-desert` | DesertFestival's Condition is 'LOCATION_ACCESSIBLE Desert'; UpdatePassiveFestivalStates only activates a passive festival whose Condition passes, and LOCATION_ACCESSIBLE calls isLocationAccessible. | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition) DesertFestival.Condition` | verified |
| `access.mention-krobus-sewer` | Krobus' Home is Sewer; Sandy's Home is SandyHouse (Desert); Skull Cavern, Oasis and Desert Trader are in the Desert; the IslandTrade shop is on Ginger Island. | `Data/Characters (BirthSeason, BirthDay, CanBeRomanced, Home, UnlockConditions, CanSocialize) Home`<br>`Data/Shops IslandTrade, DesertTrade, Sandy` | verified |
| `cal.season-order` | Season enum order spring, summer, fall, winter. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade` | verified |
| `cal.28-day-season` | 28 days per season, 112 per year; wrap by 112. |  | verified |
| `cal.weekday-formula` | shortDayNameFromDayOfSeason uses dayOfMonth % 7 (1=Mon ... 0=Sun). Since 28 is a multiple of 7, (season*28 + day - 1) % 7 gives the same weekday; day 1 of every season is a Monday. | `StardewValley.Game1.shortDayNameFromDayOfSeason` | verified |
| `cal.shop-closing-festivals` | AreStoresClosedForFestival is true on an active festival day whose start time (Data/Festivals/<id> 'conditions' second field) is before 1900. Egg, Flower Dance, Luau, Ice and Winter Star start at 900 and the Fair at 900; Moonlight Jellies and Spirit's Eve start at 2200, so shops stay open. Passive festivals are not 'festival days'. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.AreStoresClosedForFestival`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.getStartTimeOfFestival`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.isFestivalDay`<br>`Data/Festivals/<season><day>.conditions (location/start end)` | verified |
| `cal.festival-closes-all-shops` | On those days lockedDoorWarp refuses every locked shop door in the valley context (even with the Town Key). All six planner shop keys (Clint, Willy, Robin, Marnie, Pierre, Museum) sit behind such doors. Shops outside the valley context (Desert, Ginger Island) and Krobus' sewer are not affected. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.AreStoresClosedForFestival`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.lockedDoorWarp` | verified |
| `qos.episode-formula` | The Sunday Queen of Sauce episode is (DaysPlayed % 224) / 7, with a remainder of 0 meaning 32: the 32 episodes air on Year 1-2 Sundays and the same cycle restarts every two years (Year 3 Spring 7 is episode 1 again), so a Year-N Sunday matches Year ((N-1) mod 2)+1. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe` | verified |
| `qos.two-year-cycle` | The Sunday Queen of Sauce episode is (DaysPlayed % 224) / 7, with a remainder of 0 meaning 32: the 32 episodes air on Year 1-2 Sundays and the same cycle restarts every two years (Year 3 Spring 7 is episode 1 again), so a Year-N Sunday matches Year ((N-1) mod 2)+1. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `qos.wednesday-rerun` | The TV offers the cooking channel on Wednesday only when DaysPlayed > 7 (so not Spring 3 Y1). The rerun episode is picked once per day by a day-save seeded random from episodes 1..min((DaysPlayed-3)/7, 32), preferring ones whose recipe some player still lacks; it teaches that recipe too. With nothing unknown it takes 1 + Next(count) over all aired weeks. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.checkForAction`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getRerunWeek`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `qos.recipe-field0` | The recipe name is CookingChannel[id] split on '/' field 0; field 1 is the show text. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getRerunWeek`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `fest.desert-festival-gated` | Desert Festival is only active when the desert is accessible (passive festival Condition). | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `passive.desert-festival` | DesertFestival: Spring 15-17, StartTime 1000, Condition LOCATION_ACCESSIBLE Desert. | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `passive.trout-derby` | TroutDerby: Summer 20-21, StartTime 610, no condition (ShowOnCalendar false). | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `passive.squidfest` | SquidFest: Winter 12-13, StartTime 610, no condition (ShowOnCalendar false). | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `passive.night-market` | NightMarket: Winter 15-17, StartTime 1700. | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `quest.year1-only` | Story quest letters are keyed '<season>_<day>_1', and the game only adds a dated letter when the key '<season>_<day>_<year>' exists, so they are only offered in Year 1. But these ItemDelivery quests have no deadline, so one accepted in Year 1 and not finished stays in the journal into Year 2. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`Data/Quests`<br>`Data/mail` | to fix |
| `cal.birthday-wrap-112` | Birthdays recur yearly on BirthSeason/BirthDay; modulo-112 arithmetic matches. | `StardewValley.NPC.isBirthday`<br>`Data/Characters (BirthSeason, BirthDay, CanBeRomanced, Home, UnlockConditions, CanSocialize) BirthSeason/BirthDay` | verified |
| `cal.advance-wraps-112` | Matches the game's 112-day year of four 28-day seasons. |  | verified |
| `qos.weekly-episode` | Watching the cooking channel on Sunday shows that week's episode and adds its recipe if the player doesn't know it; if known, it just shows the episode. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `cal.forage-windows-calendar` | The two windows in calendar_index (salmonberry Spring 15-18, blackberry Fall 8-11) match Bush.inBloom exactly, inclusive. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.inBloom` | verified |
| `unlock.mines-landslide-mail` | landslideDone is the flag Mountain.DayUpdate sends when the landslide clears. | `StardewValley.Locations.Mountain.DayUpdate` | verified |
| `wizard.tower-location` | The Wizard's tower door is a Forest tile (index 901) handled by Forest.checkAction, so the tower is in (western) Cindersap Forest. The exact map position is in the map file, not code. | `StardewValley.Locations.Forest.checkAction` | verified |
| `quest.mail-key-format` | Each morning the game adds '<season>_<day>_<year>' to the mailbox if Data/mail has that key; the story letters end in '_1', so they are Year 1. Their text carries '%item quest <id>' (Willy's has '%item quest 13 true'). | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`Data/mail` | verified |

| `fest.summer` | summer11 Luau, summer28 Dance of the Moonlight Jellies; TroutDerby Summer 20-21. | `StardewValley.Game1.UpdatePassiveFestivalStates`<br>`Data/Festivals/FestivalDates + Data/Festivals/<season><day>.conditions`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |

| `qos.year1-schedule` | CookingChannel 1-16 in order: Stir Fry, Coleslaw, Radish Salad, Omelet, Baked Fish, Pancakes, Maki Roll, Bread, Tortilla, Trout Soup, Glazed Yams, Artichoke Dip, Plum Pudding, Chocolate Cake, Pumpkin Pie, Cranberry Candy, on Sundays day 7/14/21/28 of each season of Year 1. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `cal.birthdays-shown` | The calendar lists a villager's birthday (Data/Characters BirthSeason/BirthDay) only if they exist (UnlockConditions: Kent from Year 2, Leo once he's moved to the valley) and their Calendar setting allows it: HiddenAlways never, HiddenUntilMet once you've met them (the Dwarf, Sandy, Krobus). | `StardewValley.Menus.Billboard.GetBirthdays`<br>`Data/Characters BirthSeason, BirthDay, Calendar, UnlockConditions` | verified |

## NPCs & schedules & gifts

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `npc.hearts-250` | Heart level = friendship points / 250 (integer). | `StardewValley.Farmer.getFriendshipHeartLevelForNPC` | verified |
| `npc.hearts-250-history` | Same 250-points-per-heart rule. | `StardewValley.Farmer.getFriendshipHeartLevelForNPC` | verified |
| `npc.gift-talk-raises-points` | Talking gives +20 once a day (60 with the Statue of Blessings 'friendship' buff) and gifts change points by taste, so changes show up in the next save. But points can also fall or be capped. | `StardewValley.Farmer.changeFriendship`<br>`StardewValley.Farmer.resetFriendshipsForNewDay`<br>`StardewValley.Farmer.updateFriendshipGifts`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.grantConversationFriendship`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.receiveGift` | to fix |
| `quest.jodi-cauliflower-spring19` | Mail spring_19_1 carries quest 101: ItemDelivery to Jodi of (O)190 Cauliflower. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`Data/Quests 101`<br>`Data/mail spring_19_1` | verified |
| `gift.loved-hold-count` | How many loved gifts to keep is planner policy; no game rule decides it. |  | unverifiable |
| `gift.birthday-week-hold` | Birthdays come from Data/Characters BirthSeason/BirthDay (NPC.isBirthday compares season and day). The 8-day look-ahead is planner policy. | `StardewValley.NPC.isBirthday`<br>`Data/Characters (BirthSeason, BirthDay, CanBeRomanced, Home, UnlockConditions, CanSocialize) BirthSeason/BirthDay` | verified |
| `sched.location-access` | Inside a schedule the game only checks three places: JojaMart and Railroad (use '<Loc>_Replacement' if present, else fall back) and CommunityCenter (always fall back while not accessible). The accessibility rules (Railroad DaysPlayed > 31, CC event 191393, JojaMart until any player saw 191393) are right. | `StardewValley.Game1.isLocationAccessible`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.changeScheduleForLocationAccessibility`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.parseMasterScheduleImpl` | verified |
| `sched.replacement-spot` | Only JojaMart and Railroad entries use '<Location>_Replacement' ('location x y facing'). For CommunityCenter the game ignores any replacement and drops to the fallback schedule. The data has CommunityCenter_Replacement for Clint, Maru and Penny, which the game never uses; JojaMart_Replacement exists for Jodi, Pam and Sam; no NPC has Railroad_Replacement. | `StardewValley.NPC.changeScheduleForLocationAccessibility`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.parseMasterScheduleImpl`<br>`Characters/schedules/<NPC> *_Replacement keys` | verified |
| `sched.fallback-default` | On a blocked entry with no usable replacement, parsing restarts with 'default' if the NPC has it, else 'spring'. Edge case: the fallback goes through the same check; if it would loop back to a key already visited, the game returns an empty schedule (the NPC stays put). It does not try the next candidate key, which the planner does. | `StardewValley.NPC.changeScheduleForLocationAccessibility`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.parseMasterScheduleImpl`<br>`Characters/schedules/<NPC>` | verified |
| `sched.marriage-job-days` | Married Penny tries marriageJob on Tue/Wed/Fri, Maru and Harvey on Tue/Thu; hard-coded by name. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.hearts-250` | Heart count for schedule keys = max(0, highest friendship points with this NPC across all players) / 250. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.GetAllPlayerFriendshipLevel` | verified |
| `sched.green-rain-y1` | First check: if Green Rain and year 1, try 'GreenRain'. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.passive-fest-keys` | For each active passive festival: married NPCs try marriage_<Id>_<n> then marriage_<Id>; others <Id>_<n> then <Id>; n = dayOfMonth - StartDay + 1. This comes after GreenRain and a Ginger Island resort visit, and before everything else. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.GetDayOfPassiveFestival`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals (Season, StartDay, EndDay, StartTime, Condition)` | verified |
| `sched.married-order` | Married: marriage_<season>_<day>; then marriageJob on job days; then marriage_<weekday> only if not raining (Game1.isRaining); otherwise no schedule. GreenRain, resort-visit (islandScheduleName) and passive-festival keys come before this. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.unmarried-order` | The order matches: <season>_<day>; <day>_<h> for h=hearts..1; <day>; Pam 'bus' if the current player has ccVault; if raining, 50% rain2 then rain; <season>_<weekday>_<h> for h=hearts,hearts-2,..>0; <season>_<weekday>; <weekday>_<h> same steps; <weekday>; <season>; spring_<weekday>; spring. Keys match case-insensitively. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.pam-bus` | Pam tries 'bus' when Game1.player has mail ccVault (host's mail is used for the bus itself). | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.rain2-coinflip` | rain2 is tried first only if Game1.random.NextBool() (unseeded, so not predictable); otherwise 'rain'. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule` | verified |
| `sched.empty-entries-skip` | TryLoadSchedule(key) returns success as soon as the key exists in the NPC's schedule data, whatever the parse gives. An empty or failed parse leaves the NPC with no or empty schedule; the game does not move on to the next candidate key. | `StardewValley.NPC.TryLoadSchedule`<br>`StardewValley.NPC.parseMasterScheduleImpl` | verified |

## Community Center & bundles & route

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cc.junimo-text-mail` | canReadJunimoText is added when the Wizard event (Data/Events/WizardHouse 112, precondition: mail flag seenJunimoNote) ends. seenJunimoNote is added the first time any Junimo note menu opens (the golden scroll in the Crafts Room is the first). ccDoorUnlock is added by the Lewis Community Center event 611439 in Data/Events/Town. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.setUpMenu`<br>`Data/Events/Town 611439 script`<br>`Data/Events/Town: 611439`<br>`Data/Events/WizardHouse: 112` | verified |
| `cc.quest-prep-plus-bundle` | Donating consumes the item (tryToDepositThisItem consumes the stack) and delivering a quest item consumes it too, so one copy cannot serve both; an extra copy is needed. | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.tryToDepositThisItem` | verified |
| `cc.complete-flag` | areasComplete is a 6-entry bool array on the CommunityCenter; ccIsComplete is added to mailReceived when the last Junimo returns to its hut after all six areas are done. Either signal is valid; hasCompletedCommunityCenter() instead checks the six ccX mail flags. | `StardewValley.Characters.Junimo.returnToJunimoHutToFetchStar`<br>`StardewValley.Farmer.hasCompletedCommunityCenter`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.areAllAreasComplete` | verified |
| `cc.deadline-winter-28` | Planning choice: the game has no Community Center deadline. |  | planning choice |
| `cc.vault-excluded-history` | Vault bundles are paid with a purchase button (whichArea 4), never with items. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.setUpBundleSpecificPage` | verified |
| `cc.winter-exit-checklist` | Matches the standard Data/Bundles: Crafts Room = Spring/Summer/Fall/Winter Foraging, Construction, Exotic Foraging; Pantry = Spring/Summer/Fall Crops, Quality Crops, Animal, Artisan; Fish Tank = River, Lake, Ocean, Night Fishing, Specialty, Crab Pot; Boiler Room = Blacksmith's, Geologist's, Adventurer's; Vault = 2,500/5,000/10,000/25,000g; Bulletin Board = Chef's, Field Research, Enchanter's, Dye, Fodder. On a remixed farm the room contents differ (Data/RandomBundles), so read the save's bundleData. | `StardewValley.Game1.GenerateBundles`<br>`StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData`<br>`Data/Bundles keys and field [0]`<br>`Data/RandomBundles` | verified |

| `cc.confidence-80` | Planning choice (risk tolerance), not a game rule. |  | planning choice |
| `cc.luck-trial-model` | Planning choice. Game notes: the Cart's stock is re-rolled from a day-seeded random each visit (independent trials is fair). | `cc.vault-gold-nodes` | The bus does need all four Vault bundles: finishing the Vault area queues mail ccVault for the next morning, and BusStop checks that flag. The game does not read the bundle name for the price. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.doAreaCompleteReward`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick`<br>`Data/Bundles Vault/N field [2]` | to fix |
| `cc.cart-guarantee-y1` | In 1.6, a new game with the "Year One Completable" option whose bundles contain item 266 (Red Cabbage) sets VisitsUntilY1Guarantee = random.Next(2, 31), seeded from the game id. The counter goes down by 1 on every Forest cart day (day-of-month %7 %5 == 0, i.e. 5,7,12,14,19,21,26,28) and on every Night Market day, while it is >= 0. The Traveler shop stocks Red Cabbage Seeds (O)485 only on the visit when it equals 0. The shop condition does not check the year, but 30 visits always run out within Year 1. The save's value already includes today's decrement, so 0 means today; it goes down on each later Forest cart day and Night Market day (not Desert Festival days) while >= 0, and -1 means off or used. | `StardewValley.Locations.Forest.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.ShouldTravelingMerchantVisitToday`<br>`Data/Shops Traveler item (O)485 Condition "WORLD_STATE_FIELD VisitsUntilY1Guarantee 0"`<br>`save: visitsUntilY1Guarantee` | verified |

| `cc.save-bundle-data` | SaveGame writes netWorldState.BundleData into <bundleData>, and on load SetBundleData restores it. A new game builds it from Data/Bundles (standard) or BundleGenerator over Data/RandomBundles (remixed), using the same string format. | `StardewValley.BundleGenerator.Generate`<br>`StardewValley.BundleGenerator.ParseItemList`<br>`StardewValley.BundleGenerator.ParseItemString`<br>`StardewValley.Game1.GenerateBundles`<br>`StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData`<br>`Data/Bundles`<br>`Data/RandomBundles`<br>`save <bundleData>` | verified |
| `cc.bundle-string-format` | Value fields split on "/": [0] internal name, [1] reward, [2] space-separated triples (id count quality), [3] color index, [4] slots needed (empty/missing means all ingredients), [5] optional sprite override, [6] display name used in the UI. The save's per-bundle bool array has one entry per token (3 per ingredient); ingredient i uses entry i. | `StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData`<br>`Data/Bundles value`<br>`save <bundleData>` | verified |
| `cc.abandoned-skip` | "Abandoned Joja Mart/36" (The Missing Bundle) is area 6. Its note appears only after event 191393 (the CC completion ceremony) has been seen, so it is not part of the CC. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.getAreaNumberFromName`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.shouldNoteAppearInArea`<br>`Data/Bundles Abandoned Joja Mart/36` | verified |
| `cc.gold-item-id` | A negative ingredient id is an item category (remixed bundles use -5 eggs, -6 milk, etc.). Gold is decided by the area: Vault bundles (area 4) are bought with the purchase button for the stack of their last ingredient. | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.BundleIngredientDescription.BundleIngredientDescription`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick`<br>`Data/Bundles Vault/N`<br>`Data/RandomBundles items "10 EggCategory, 10 MilkCategory"` | verified |
| `cc.quality-codes` | The bundle quality token is compared directly with the item's quality: the slot rejects an item when the required value is greater than item.Quality. Data uses 0 and 2. The remix generator maps NQ/SQ/GQ/IQ to 0/1/2/3. Item quality 3 does not exist, so a 3 accepts only iridium (4). | `StardewValley.BundleGenerator.ParseItemString`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription`<br>`Data/Bundles field [2] third token of each triple` | verified |
| `cc.quality-min-map` | Item quality values are 0/1/2/4. A bundle value of 3 or 4 can only be met by an iridium item, so treating "iridium" as min 4 is equivalent. | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription` | verified |
| `cc.quality-at-least` | A quality slot accepts any item whose quality is at least the required value. Using up the lowest qualifying quality first is the planner's own policy (in game the player picks which stack). | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription` | verified |
| `cc.room-order` | This order matches the order in which notes unlock (thresholds 0/1/1/2/3/4 complete bundles in shouldNoteAppearInArea). The game's own area numbers are different: Pantry 0, Crafts Room 1, Fish Tank 2, Boiler Room 3, Vault 4, Bulletin Board 5, Abandoned Joja Mart 6. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.getAreaNumberFromName`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.shouldNoteAppearInArea` | verified |
| `cc.bundle-key-index` | SetBundleData keys the per-bundle bool arrays (and bundleRewards) by the integer after "/" in the bundleData key. | `StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData` | verified |
| `cc.complete-by-need` | Bundle ctor: the bundle is complete when the number of completed ingredients is at least numberOfIngredientSlots (field [4], else the ingredient count). When an item bundle completes, every flag in its save array is set true. Vault bundles set only flag [0]. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.checkIfBundleIsComplete`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick` | verified |
| `cc.one-item-one-slot` | Depositing consumes the stack (ConsumeStack) and marks one ingredient slot done, so a physical item can fill only one slot. | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.tryToDepositThisItem` | verified |
| `cc.full-stack-only` | This reservation policy fits the game. Since 1.6 HandlePartialDonation lets you top up one slot from several qualifying stacks (mixed qualities, all at least the required one) in one sitting, but ReturnPartialDonations hands an unfinished slot back when the bundle check runs or the menu closes. So a slot can only be finished when enough qualifying items are in hand together. Note: donating uses only the player inventory; chest stock must be carried first. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.CanBePartiallyOrFullyDonated`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.HandlePartialDonation`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.ReturnPartialDonations` | verified |
| `cc.vault-no-items` | Vault bundles are completed with gold through the purchase button (whichArea 4); deposits are not used. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.setUpBundleSpecificPage`<br>`Data/Bundles Vault/N` | verified |
| `cc.item-match-by-name` | Bundles match by exact qualified item id (unqualified ids are objects), by category for negative ids (a Dinosaur Egg also counts for -5 eggs), and need at least the asked quality. Names are never used, so shared display names (Egg 176/180, Large Egg 174/182) can't be confused. | `StardewValley.ItemRegistry.HasItemId`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription` | verified |
| `cc.room-unlock-counts` | Notes appear: Crafts Room always, Pantry and Fish Tank after 1 complete bundle, Boiler Room after 2, Bulletin Board after 3, Vault after 4, the Joja 'Missing' bundle after the CC ceremony (event 191393); never once each bundle's ingredient flags in the room are all set. A bundle counts as complete only when every entry of its save list (3 per ingredient) is true, so paid Vault bundles (only entry 0 set) don't count. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.checkAction`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.numberOfCompleteBundles`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.shouldNoteAppearInArea`<br>`StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData` | verified |
| `cc.room-done-unlocked` | A finished area never gets its note back (shouldNoteAppearInArea returns false once every bundle there is done), so treating it as not locked is fine. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.shouldNoteAppearInArea` | verified |
| `cc.junimo-text-gate` | JunimoNoteMenu sets scrambledText when the player neither has nor will receive canReadJunimoText. receiveLeftClick returns before any deposit or purchase while scrambled. | `StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.receiveLeftClick`<br>`StardewValley.Menus.JunimoNoteMenu.setUpMenu` | verified |

## Crops & fertilizer

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `crops.hoedirt-crop` | Field crops are HoeDirt terrain features (written as TerrainFeature xsi:type="HoeDirt") with a non-nil crop. Garden pots are Objects of xsi:type IndoorPot whose dirt lives in a child element named hoeDirt; because that field's declared type is already HoeDirt, the serializer writes no xsi:type on it, so a search for xsi:type=HoeDirt misses every crop in a garden pot. | `save file XML` | verified |
| `crops.phase-days-remaining` | phaseDays is the list of days per growth phase (already shortened by Speed-Gro/Agriculturist/paddy bonus at planting) with a final 99999 marker. Each growing night dayOfCurrentPhase goes up by one (capped at the phase length); when it reaches the phase length the crop moves to the next phase at day 0, skipping zero-length phases. The crop is ready once currentPhase is the last index. So days left = (phase length - dayOfCurrentPhase) + the later real phases, exactly as the planner computes; each of those days needs the tile watered. | `StardewValley.Crop.newDay`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.readyForHarvest`<br>`save: crop/phaseDays, currentPhase, dayOfCurrentPhase` | verified |
| `crops.regrow-countdown` | After a regrowing crop is harvested the game sets fullGrown=true and dayOfCurrentPhase=RegrowDays; each watered night it decrements, and the crop is harvestable again when it is <= 0 (it stays in the last phase). So days left = max(0, dayOfCurrentPhase). | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`StardewValley.Crop.newDay`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.readyForHarvest`<br>`Data/Crops.json RegrowDays` | verified |
| `crops.index-of-harvest` | indexOfHarvest is serialized as the unqualified item id of Data/Crops HarvestItemId (e.g. 256); stripping a (O) prefix is harmless. Caveats: wild-seed crops store their real (random/seasonal) produce in overrideHarvestItemId; Sunflower's id is switched to 431 (seeds) during harvest only for the bonus drop. | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`Data/Crops.json HarvestItemId`<br>`save: crop/indexOfHarvest` | verified |
| `crops.tile-key` | Location terrainFeatures is a dictionary keyed by Vector2 tile; the HoeDirt is the value. Garden-pot dirt has no key of its own (it is inside the pot object, whose objects-dictionary key is the tile). | `save: terrainFeatures/item/key/Vector2` | verified |
| `crops.watered-state` | HoeDirt.state: 0 dry, 1 watered, 2 invisible (unused dirt). In a fresh save state 1 means the tile is already watered for that day (rain, sprinkler, retaining soil or a paddy near water set it during the overnight update). | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.dayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.isWatered`<br>`save: HoeDirt/state` | verified |
| `crops.dead-flag` | crop/dead=true marks a dead crop; readyForHarvest is false for it and harvest gives nothing. | `StardewValley.Crop.Kill`<br>`StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.readyForHarvest`<br>`save: crop/dead` | verified |
| `crops.regrows-flag` | 1.6 saves have no regrowAfterHarvest element any more (the field was removed; regrowth comes from Data/Crops RegrowDays via Crop.RegrowsAfterHarvest). fullGrown only becomes true after the first harvest of a regrowing crop. So a regrowing crop that hasn't been picked yet (fullGrown=false) is reported as non-regrowing by the planner. Confirmed: no regrowAfterHarvest in the local 1.6.15 saves. | `StardewValley.Crop.RegrowsAfterHarvest`<br>`StardewValley.Crop.growCompletely`<br>`StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`Data/Crops.json RegrowDays (key = crop/seedIndex)` | verified |
| `crops.fertilizer-field` | HoeDirt/fertilizer holds the fertilizer item id; 1.6 writes it qualified, e.g. (O)368 (seen in all local 1.6 saves), older saves may be unqualified. Only one fertilizer can be on a tile at a time. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.CheckApplyFertilizerRules`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.HasFertilizer`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.plant`<br>`save: HoeDirt/fertilizer` | verified |
| `crops.grown-so-far` | Growth days completed = sum of earlier phases + dayOfCurrentPhase while the crop is still growing; once in the final phase the first-growth count is no longer meaningful. | `StardewValley.Crop.newDay`<br>`save: crop/phaseDays, currentPhase, dayOfCurrentPhase` | verified |
| `crops.hand-water-energy` | The watering can costs stamina per use, not per tile: 2*(power+1) - 0.1*FarmingLevel, and nothing with the Efficient enchantment. A single-tile tap (power 0) costs 2 - 0.1*FarmingLevel (1.0 at Farming 10); charged uses water more tiles for 4/6/8/10 base. | `StardewValley.Tools.WateringCan.DoFunction` | to fix |
| `crops.default-tiles-60` | A planner setting (how many tiles the player can water a day), not a game rule. |  | unverifiable |

| `crop.gold-chance` | At harvest the game computes g = 0.2*(FarmingLevel/10) + 0.2*F*((FarmingLevel+2)/12) + 0.01, where F is the tile's fertilizer quality level (0 none, 1 Basic, 2 Quality, 3 Deluxe) and FarmingLevel is the effective level including food buffs. It then draws independent rolls from one tile/day-seeded RNG: only when F >= 3, an iridium roll succeeds with chance g/2; otherwise a gold roll with chance g; otherwise silver with chance min(0.75, 2g), and with F >= 3 a failed gold roll is always at least silver. The result is clamped to the crop's HarvestMinQuality/HarvestMaxQuality (Fiber and Qi Fruit are capped at normal). The planner's formula is exactly g. For no fertilizer, Basic or Quality (F<3) iridium is impossible, so g = P(gold) = P(gold or better). | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.GetFertilizerQualityBoostLevel`<br>`Data/Crops.json HarvestMinQuality/HarvestMaxQuality` | verified |

It then draws independent rolls from one tile/day-seeded RNG: only when F >= 3, an iridium roll succeeds with chance g/2; otherwise a gold roll with chance g; otherwise silver with chance min(0.75, 2g), and with F >= 3 a failed gold roll is always at least silver. The result is clamped to the crop's HarvestMinQuality/HarvestMaxQuality (Fiber and Qi Fruit are capped at normal). With Deluxe Fertilizer (F=3): P(iridium)=g/2, P(gold)=(1-g/2)*g, P(silver)=the rest, P(normal)=0. So P(gold or better) = g/2 + (1-g/2)*g = 1.5g - g^2/2, larger than g. Plugging F=3 into the gold formula alone understates gold-or-better. | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest` | verified |

| `crop.artisan-categories` | Keg: fruit (category_fruits) -> Wine at 3x price in 10000 min; vegetables (category_vegetable) -> Juice at floor(2.25x) in 6000 min. Jar: fruit -> Jelly, vegetable -> Pickles, both 2x+50 in 4000 min. But the Keg checks specific items first: Wheat -> Beer, Hops -> Pale Ale (both vegetables), Coffee Bean -> Coffee, Tea Leaves -> Green Tea, Honey -> Mead, Rice -> Vinegar; edible forage greens also make Juice/Pickles. Flowers make nothing in these machines (only flavour Honey via a Bee House). | `Data/Machines.json (BC)12 Keg and (BC)15 Preserves Jar OutputRules`<br>`Data/Objects.json Category` | to fix |
| `crop-seasons.die-unless-next-listed` | Correct that outdoor crops die at a season change unless the new season is listed. But in Data/Crops an empty Seasons list means the crop cannot be planted at all (HoeDirt.plant refuses it); no vanilla crop has []. Cactus Fruit actually lists all four seasons, and its seeds can't be planted outdoors except on Ginger Island (PlantableLocationRules). Data/Crops is now readable as JSON, so the hand-written list is unnecessary. | `StardewValley.Crop.IsInSeason`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.plant`<br>`Data/Crops.json Seasons, PlantableLocationRules` | verified |

| `crop.rain-waters` | Rain/storm/green rain water only HoeDirt in outdoor locations where it's raining (and outdoor pots). Greenhouse crops and indoor pots are never rain-watered; Ginger Island has its own weather, the Desert none. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`StardewValley.Network.LocationWeather.UpdateDailyWeather` | to fix |
| `crop.dry-no-growth` | An unwatered crop (state != 1) skips its growth step that night; it doesn't die. Crops with NeedsWatering=false (Fiber) grow anyway. | `StardewValley.Crop.newDay`<br>`Data/Crops.json NeedsWatering` | verified |
| `crop.gold-needs-basic-fert` | Fertilizer is not required for gold: with none, P(gold) = 0.02*Farming + 0.01 (e.g. 11% at Farming 5). Basic Fertilizer adds 0.2*(Farming+2)/12 to it, and must be on the tile before the crop sprouts. | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.HoeDirt.CheckApplyFertilizerRules` | to fix |
| `quest.prep-covered` | Planner bookkeeping (how many to keep for a request), not a game rule. |  | unverifiable |
| `hw.pool-crops` | Item-delivery Help Wanted quests pick a crop 15% of the time (not in Winter) from possibleCropsAtThisTime. The planner's sets match the full lists, but during days 1-7 the game uses a smaller first-week list: Spring 24, 192 (+250 Y2+, +248 with Vault); Summer 264, 262, 260 (+266 Y2+); Fall 272, 278 (+284 with Vault). From day 8 the full lists apply (Fall's 274 only from Y2, 282 with Vault). 'Y2' means year > 1. | `StardewValley.Quests.ItemDeliveryQuest.loadQuestInfo`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.possibleCropsAtThisTime` | to fix |

## Economy & shops & selling

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `econ.tiller-10` | Tiller (profession 1) multiplies the sell price by 1.1 for vegetables (-75), flowers (-80) and fruit (-79). The 10% figure is right. Two caveats: fruit flagged as spawned forage gets no bonus, and other categories (forage -81, Sweet Gem Berry -17, Coffee Bean -74) get none either. | `StardewValley.Object.getPriceAfterMultipliers`<br>`Data/Objects Category` | verified |
| `econ.artisan-40` | Artisan (profession 4) multiplies the sell price of any category -26 (artisan goods) item by 1.4. | `StardewValley.Object.getPriceAfterMultipliers`<br>`Data/Objects Category` | verified |
| `econ.total-earned-meaning` | totalMoneyEarned goes up by the gain whenever a player's money increases, from any source: shipping, selling to shops, quest rewards, mail and so on. Spending never lowers it. The only thing that takes it back down is buying an item back from a shop's buyback list. It is a single team-wide figure (FarmerTeam.totalMoneyEarned), so in multiplayer it pools every farmer's earnings. | `StardewValley.Menus.ShopMenu.BuyBuybackItem` | verified |
| `econ.cart-y1-guarantee` | visitsUntilY1Guarantee is a world-state counter. The cart's guaranteed Red Cabbage Seeds entry (stock 1, random price) only shows while it equals 0. The save's value already includes today's decrement, so 0 means today; it goes down on each later Forest cart day and Night Market day (not Desert Festival days) while >= 0, and -1 means off or used. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.DayUpdate`<br>`Data/Shops Traveler item (O)485 Condition 'WORLD_STATE_FIELD VisitsUntilY1Guarantee 0 0'` | verified |
| `econ.income-window` | Planner policy/arithmetic, not a game rule; nothing in the game code or data to check it against. The input it uses (totalMoneyEarned) is checked under econ.total-earned-meaning. |  | unverifiable |
| `econ.afford-day` | Planner policy/arithmetic, not a game rule; nothing in the game code or data to check it against. |  | unverifiable |
| `econ.gold-batch-limit` | Planner policy/arithmetic, not a game rule; nothing in the game code or data to check it against. |  | unverifiable |
| `econ.budget-week` | Planner policy/arithmetic, not a game rule; nothing in the game code or data to check it against. |  | unverifiable |
| `econ.copper-axe` | Clint's copper upgrade costs 2,000g plus 5 Copper Bars. The Tools data has no explicit upgrade cost, so the price and bar type come from the conventional-upgrade table, which is hard-coded by level: 1 = 2,000g + Copper Bar, 2 = 5,000g + Iron Bar, 3 = 10,000g + Gold Bar, 4 = 25,000g + Iridium Bar, always 5 bars. The tool is ready after 2 days (daysLeftForToolUpgrade = 2). | `StardewValley.Internal.ShopBuilder.GetToolUpgradeConventionalPrice`<br>`StardewValley.Internal.ShopBuilder.GetToolUpgradeData`<br>`StardewValley.Tool.actionWhenPurchased`<br>`Data/Shops ClintUpgrade (TOOL_UPGRADES)`<br>`Data/Tools CopperAxe.ConventionalUpgradeFrom/UpgradeLevel/UpgradeFrom` | verified |
| `econ.steel-axe` | The steel upgrade is level 2: 5,000g + 5 Iron Bars, 2 days. The Copper Axe must be in the player's inventory (RequireToolId = (T)CopperAxe). | `StardewValley.Internal.ShopBuilder.GetToolUpgradeConventionalPrice`<br>`StardewValley.Internal.ShopBuilder.GetToolUpgradeData`<br>`StardewValley.Tool.actionWhenPurchased`<br>`Data/Tools SteelAxe.ConventionalUpgradeFrom = (T)CopperAxe` | verified |
| `econ.kitchen` | The first house upgrade costs 10,000g + 450 Wood and takes 3 days. These values are hard-coded in the upgrade handler; no Data asset holds them. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.houseUpgradeAccept`<br>`(none - hard-coded)` | verified |

| `econ.red-cabbage-cart` | The cart's random slot picks 10 distinct objects from a pool of about 334, so any one item (Red Cabbage 266 or Red Cabbage Seeds 485) has about a 3.0% chance per visit (10/334). The odds do not change with the season. The pool is objects with numeric IDs 2-789 that have a negative category other than -999, a sale price, are not excluded from random sales, and are not Quest/Minerals/Arch type. The chance of at least one of the two is about 5.9%, which matches the planned 6%. The guaranteed seeds entry is separate (see econ.cart-y1-guarantee). | `StardewValley.Internal.ItemQueryResolver.DefaultResolvers.RANDOM_ITEMS`<br>`StardewValley.Internal.ShopBuilder.GetShopStock`<br>`Data/Objects Category/Type/Price/ExcludeFromRandomSale`<br>`Data/Shops Traveler RandomObjects (RANDOM_ITEMS (O) 2 789, MaxItems 10, PerItemCondition)` | verified |

## Fishing

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fish.rod-gift-spring-2` | Willy's letter (mail key spring_2_1) arrives on Spring 2 of Year 1 and the Beach event 739330 then hands over a Bamboo Pole. But a rod can be owned earlier: the Fish Shop sells a Training Rod for 25g with no condition, and the shop is open from Spring 1. | `Data/Events/Beach event 739330 (time 600-1710, needs mail spring_2_1); Data/mail spring_2_1; Data/TriggerActions Mail_Willy_ResendSpring21`<br>`Data/Shops[FishShop] (T)TrainingRod price 25, no condition` | to fix |
| `fish.rain-bite` | Fish marked 'rainy' check whether it is raining at the location. Rain and storm set IsRaining. Green rain sets it too, because setting IsGreenRain also turns on IsRaining. Snow does not set it. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.CheckGenericFishRequirements`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.IsRainingHere`<br>`StardewValley.Network.LocationWeather.UpdateDailyWeather`<br>`Data/Fish (fields: 1 difficulty, 5 time spans, 6 seasons, 7 weather, 12 min level; 'trap' rows: 2 chance, 4 water type)` | verified |
| `fish.gated-locations` | The three gates hold: Secret Woods (Steel Axe log), Desert (bus) and the Mines (open from DaysPlayed 5, i.e. Spring 5). The map is incomplete, though. Other fish-bearing spots also need unlocks: Sewer (Rusty Key), BugLand/Mutant Bug Lair (Dark Talisman quest), Witch's Swamp (later quest), Submarine (Night Market only, Winter 15-17) and Railroad (DaysPlayed >= 31, though it has no fish). | `StardewValley.Locations.Mountain.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.ResourceClump.performToolAction`<br>`Data/Locations[*].Fish (ItemId/RandomItemId, Season, Condition LOCATION_SEASON, FishAreaId, IgnoreFishDataRequirements, RequireMagicBait, CatchLimit, MinFishingLevel)`<br>`Data/PassiveFestivals[NightMarket]` | to fix |
| `fish.gate-only-if-all-spots` | The 'gated only if every spot is gated' logic is sound. Farm_* fish data applies only on a farm of that type: the Farm getFish uses that farm type's data, and some farms add a FarmFishLocationOverride. So the spots are unavailable on other farm types, not on every save. | `StardewValley.Farm.getFish`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.GetFishFromLocationData`<br>`Data/Locations[*].Fish (ItemId/RandomItemId, Season, Condition LOCATION_SEASON, FishAreaId, IgnoreFishDataRequirements, RequireMagicBait, CatchLimit, MinFishingLevel) (Farm_Beach, Farm_Forest, Farm_FourCorners, Farm_Hilltop, Farm_Riverland, Farm_Wilderness, Farm_MeadowlandsFarm)` | verified |
| `fish.island-excluded` | Planning choice: Ginger Island spots ignored for CC planning. That is reasonable, because the island needs Willy's boat, offered only once the CC or Joja route is complete (trigger Mail_Willy_BackRoomUnlocked). | `Data/TriggerActions Mail_Willy_BackRoomUnlocked (ANY IS_COMMUNITY_CENTER_COMPLETE IS_JOJA_MART_COMPLETE)` | planning choice |
| `fish.crab-pot-lead` | A crab pot rolls its catch in its overnight DayUpdate, so the catch is ready the next morning. It needs bait unless the owner has Luremaster (profession 11). Rod fish are caught the same day. | `StardewValley.Objects.CrabPot.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.CrabPot.NeedsBait`<br>`Data/Fish (fields: 1 difficulty, 5 time spans, 6 seasons, 7 weather, 12 min level; 'trap' rows: 2 chance, 4 water type)` | verified |
| `fish.rain-chance` | Rain odds vary by season and are not a flat 20%. Spring/fall: SYNCED_RANDOM 0.183 per day, with storms as a subset. Summer: 0.12 + 0.003 x day-of-month (about 12-20%), plus storms always on Summer 13 and 26 and one green-rain day. Winter: never rains naturally; it snows 63% of days instead. Forced sun: day 1 of every season, festival days and Night Market days. Year 1 Spring 1-4 is fixed, with rain only on Spring 3. | `StardewValley.Game1.getWeatherModificationsForDate`<br>`StardewValley.GameStateQuery.SYNCED_SUMMER_RAIN_RANDOM`<br>`StardewValley.Object.rainTotem`<br>`Data/LocationContexts[Default].WeatherConditions; Data/LocationContexts[Island/Desert].SeasonOverride` | to fix |
| `fish.sun-chance` | Sunny-only fish fail only when IsRaining is true, and snow counts as dry. So the dry share is about 82% in spring and fall, about 80-88% in summer, and 100% in winter, not a flat 80%. | `StardewValley.Game1.getWeatherModificationsForDate`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.CheckGenericFishRequirements`<br>`Data/LocationContexts[Default].WeatherConditions; Data/LocationContexts[Island/Desert].SeasonOverride` | to fix |

| `fish.beach-forage-odds` | The Beach entries Clam 0.9 / Cockle 0.1 / Mussel 0.3 / Oyster 0.3 are per-attempt acceptance chances, not daily odds. | `fish.location-season-limit` | Location season limits are right, but intersecting them with Data/Fish seasons is wrong. | `fish.island-always-summer` | The Island context does override the season to Summer (Desert does too). The planner's rule drops Midnight Carp, which Data/Fish lists as fall/winter, from IslandWest and IslandNorth. | `StardewValley.Game1.GetSeasonForLocation`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.GetFishFromLocationData`<br>`Data/LocationContexts[Default].WeatherConditions; Data/LocationContexts[Island/Desert].SeasonOverride`<br>`Data/Locations[*].Fish (ItemId/RandomItemId, Season, Condition LOCATION_SEASON, FishAreaId, IgnoreFishDataRequirements, RequireMagicBait, CatchLimit, MinFishingLevel)` | verified |
| `fish.mines-code-spawn` | Mine fish are hard-coded by mine area: area 0/10 gives Stonefish, area 40 Ice Pip, area 80 Lava Eel. Water exists on floors 20, 60 and 100. The chance grows with fishing level, water depth, the curiosity lure and matching targeted bait. A Training Rod in the Mines only ever catches trash. | `StardewValley.Locations.MineShaft.getFish`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.MineShaft.getMineArea`<br>`Data/Locations[UndergroundMine].Fish (fallback: Ghostfish, algae, Cave Jelly)` | verified |
| `fish.submarine-winter` | The Submarine is reachable only during the Night Market, Winter 15-17 from 17:00. | `fish.event-spawns-excluded` | Those spawns are real but conditional: magic bait (Beach Midnight Squid/Spook Fish/Blobfish), festival-only (Temp/Ice Festival, SquidFest, Trout Derby) or the Qi LEGENDARY_FAMILY order. ## Farm cave & misc

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `misc.farm-cave-choice` | caveChoice is 0 until the event; picking mushrooms sets 2 and fruit bats sets 1. | `StardewValley.Event.hostActionChooseCave`<br>`Data/Events/Farm event 65 'm 25000 / t 600 1200 / H'` | verified |
| `misc.demetrius-cave-25000` | Event 65 requires totalMoneyEarned >= 25000. That stat counts every money increase and is shared by the team. | `StardewValley.Preconditions.EarnedMoney`<br>`Data/Events/Farm event 65 'm 25000 / t 600 1200 / H'` | verified |
| `misc.shop-milestones` | Planning choice (how the planner infers purchases). |  | planning choice |
| `misc.default-packs` | Planning choice. |  | planning choice |
| `misc.object-ids-table` | Of the 269 ids, 267 match Data/Objects names. Two are wrong: 223 is 'Cookie', not 'Cookies', and 374 doesn't exist in 1.6. | `Data/Objects (Name, Type, Category, ContextTags) Name` | to fix |
| `misc.object-id-374` | Object 374 is not in Data/Objects in 1.6. Copper Ore is 378 only. | `Data/Objects (Name, Type, Category, ContextTags)` | to fix |
| `misc.book-ids` | Most book mappings are wrong. Data/Objects names: Book_Void = Monster Compendium, Book_Bombs = Dwarvish Safety Manual, Book_Defense = Jack Be Nimble, Jack Be Thick, Book_Mystery = Book of Mysteries, Book_Trash = The Alleyway Buffet. SkillBook_0 = Stardew Valley Almanac (farming), _1 = Bait And Bobber (fishing), _2 = Woodcutter's Weekly (foraging), _3 = Mining Monthly, _4 = Combat Quarterly. Book_Crabbing, Book_PriceCatalogue, Book_Roe, Book_Woodcutting and Raisins are correct. BasiliskPaw, FairyBox, FrogEgg and ParrotEgg are trinkets (Data/Trinkets), not objects. | `Data/Objects (Name, Type, Category, ContextTags) Name/DisplayName`<br>`Strings/Objects` | to fix |

| `misc.recipe-mushroom-log` | The recipe (10 Hardwood + 10 Moss) and the 3-day cycle are right. | `misc.mushroom-log-odds` | The output is picked uniformly from a candidate list. Each mature tree within the 7x7 area adds one candidate by type: oak gives Morel, maple Red (90%) or Purple (10%), pine Chanterelle, mystic tree Purple, any other tree the generic roll. The generic roll is Purple 5%, else Red 15%, else Common. On top of that come max(1, floor(0.75 x all trees counted)) generic candidates. The fixed 40/5/50% figures are approximations that depend on the tree mix. | `StardewValley.Object.OutputMushroomLog`<br>`Data/Machines (OutputRules MinutesUntilReady/DaysUntilReady, OutputItem Condition) [(BC)MushroomLog]` | to fix |
| `misc.tapper-days` | Oak 7 days (Oak Resin), Maple 9 (Maple Syrup), Pine 5 (Pine Tar). The Heavy Tapper halves these via the tapper_multiplier context tag. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.TryGetTapperOutput`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.UpdateTapperProduct`<br>`Data/WildTrees[*].TapItems (ItemId, DaysUntilReady)` | verified |
| `misc.machine-times` | Bee House: 4 days, not in winter (condition !LOCATION_SEASON Target Winter). Jelly: 4000 minutes (about 3 days). Wine: 10000 minutes (about 7 days). | `Data/Machines (OutputRules MinutesUntilReady/DaysUntilReady, OutputItem Condition) [(BC)10, (BC)15 Default_Jelly, (BC)12 Default_Wine]` | verified |
| `misc.cave-mushrooms` | The cave gets 6 Mushroom Boxes (plus a Dehydrator), each producing daily. Rules are tried in order: Purple 2.5%, Chanterelle 7.5%, Morel 9%, Red 15%, else Common. Per box that is Purple 2.5%, Chanterelle 7.3%, Morel 8.1%, Red 12.3%, Common 69.7%. Over 6 boxes the daily chance of at least one is Red 54.5%, Purple 14.1% and Morel 39.9%, matching the planner. Common is about 99.9%, not 90%. A box holding an uncollected mushroom doesn't roll again. | `StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.setUpMushroomHouse`<br>`Data/Machines (OutputRules MinutesUntilReady/DaysUntilReady, OutputItem Condition) [(BC)128]` | to fix |
| `misc.cave-bats` | Each night the game keeps spawning while a 0.66 roll succeeds, about 1.94 fruit on average. Each fruit is 1/5 each Salmonberry, Spice Berry, Wild Plum or Blackberry. The last fifth is Apple 10%, else one of Apricot, Orange, Peach, Pomegranate or Cherry. Daily chance of at least one: berry 28.0%, each tree fruit 6.5%, Apple 3.7%, matching the planner. A spawn is lost if its random tile is occupied, so uncollected fruit reduces later yield. | `StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.setUpMushroomHouse` | verified |
| `misc.cave-lead` | Both products are rolled in the overnight DayUpdate, so the first output arrives the morning after the choice. | `StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.DayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.FarmCave.setUpMushroomHouse` | verified |
| `misc.green-rain` | One summer day per year, chosen deterministically per year and save from 5, 6, 7, 14, 15, 16, 18 or 23. On that day the Forest spawns temporary fern trees (type 12) on its GreenRain path tiles. Other green-rain locations spawn mossy trees, with ferns at 10%. A tapped fern gives Fiddlehead Fern after 2 days. When the tree reverts the next non-green-rain day (fern to Maple), the tapper keeps the Fiddlehead it already holds, as the note inferred. The modelled window of days 7-25 doesn't match the possible days. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.performGreenRainUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.TryGetTapperOutput`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.UpdateTapperProduct`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.dayUpdate`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Tree.onGreenRainDay`<br>`StardewValley.Utility.isGreenRainDay`<br>`Data/LocationContexts[Default].WeatherConditions; Data/LocationContexts[Island/Desert].SeasonOverride GreenRain`<br>`Data/WildTrees[*].TapItems (ItemId, DaysUntilReady) [12] Fiddlehead 2 days` | to fix |
