## Save file

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `save.seasons-order` | SaveGameInfo seasonForSaveGame is a nullable int written from Game1.seasonIndex at the start of each new day (0 spring, 1 summer, 2 fall, 3 winter); the main save's root currentSeason is the lowercase season name. | `StardewValley.Game1._newDayAfterFade`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.quality-codes` | Object quality constants are 0 normal, 1 silver, 2 gold, 4 iridium; 3 is never assigned. |  | verified |
| `save.gold-plus-quality` | With the 0/1/2/4 scale, quality >= 2 is exactly gold or iridium. |  | verified |
| `save.fruit-tree-treeid-1.6` | 1.6 FruitTree stores treeId = the sapling's object id, which is the key of Data/FruitTrees; each entry's Fruit list gives the fruit (628 Cherry 638, 629 Apricot 634, 630 Orange 635, 631 Peach 636, 632 Pomegranate 637, 633 Apple 613, 69 Banana 91, 835 Mango 834). | `Data/FruitTrees -> key (sapling id), Fruit[].ItemId, Seasons`<br>`Data/Objects -> Name` | verified |
| `save.fruit-tree-index-legacy` | Pre-1.6 saves carry treeType (0-8) and indexOfFruit; the fruit ids listed match the fruit each tree produces (613 Apple, 634 Apricot, 635 Orange, 636 Peach, 637 Pomegranate, 638 Cherry, 91 Banana, 834 Mango). The 1.6 migrator maps treeType 0,1,2,3,4,5,7,8 to saplings 628,629,630,631,632,633,69,835. | `Data/FruitTrees -> Fruit[].ItemId (reverse map fruit -> tree)`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.fruit-tree-mature` | FruitTree.daysUntilMature defaults to 28 and drops by growthRate each day (only when the tree isn't blocked); growth stage is derived from it and the tree is fully grown (stage 4) once it is <= 0. It keeps going negative afterwards (used for fruit quality by age). Fruit still only appears in the tree's Seasons (or in the greenhouse/island). | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.FruitTree.DaysUntilMatureToGrowthStage`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.FruitTree.GetQuality`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.FruitTree.dayUpdate`<br>`Data/FruitTrees -> Seasons` | verified |
| `save.cc-area-order` | CommunityCenter.areasComplete is a 6-element bool array indexed 0 Pantry, 1 Crafts Room, 2 Fish Tank, 3 Boiler Room, 4 Vault, 5 Bulletin Board (area ids 6-8, Abandoned Joja Mart etc., are not in the array). An entry is set during the room-restore cutscene. | `StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.getAreaNumberFromName`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.CommunityCenter.markAreaAsComplete`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.bundles-dict` | The CommunityCenter location serializes <bundles> (backed by the world state): key = bundle index (the number after the slash in the bundleData key), value = bool array whose length is the number of space-separated tokens in the ingredient field, i.e. 3 x ingredient count. The real save has 31 entries; the >=20 heuristic finds it, though matching the CommunityCenter GameLocation directly is more robust. | `StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.bundle-data-1.6` | SaveGame.bundleData holds this farm's bundle definitions keyed 'Room/index' in Data/Bundles format (name/reward/ingredients/colour/slots/sprite/display name). It has been in saves since remixed bundles arrived (1.5); a save without it uses Data/Bundles. | `StardewValley.Network.NetWorldState.SetBundleData`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.item-xsi-type-object` | Inventory and chest items are <Item xsi:type=...>. Plain objects are xsi:type 'Object' with unqualified itemId, parentSheetIndex, price and category. But 1.6 also writes Object subclasses: every preserve/artisan good with a source item (Wine, Jelly, Pickles, Juice, Dried fruit, flavoured Honey, Roe...) and coloured flowers (Poppy, Summer Spangle...) are xsi:type 'ColoredObject'. The real save holds Poppy and Summer Spangle as ColoredObject. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.item-price-field` | Object.price is the object's own base price. For flavoured goods it is set from the source item when the good is created (e.g. a Pickled Tomato in the save has price 170, not the base Pickles price), and sellToStorePrice starts from it. | `StardewValley.Object.sellToStorePrice`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.item-stack-quality` | Item.stack serializes as <stack> and quality as <quality>; a stack is always >= 1 for a real item. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.bigcraftable-flag` | Object.bigCraftable is serialized; when true the itemId is a big-craftable id (qualified '(BC)'), not an object id, so the object id/price/category must not be used. | `Data/BigCraftables`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.large-egg-brown-182` | Objects 174 and 182 are both named 'Large Egg' (same price 95, edibility 15, category -5); 182 is the brown one. Bundle ingredients match by qualified id. The Animal bundle lists 186, 182, 174, 438, 440, 442 but needs only 4 of the 6. | `StardewValley.Menus.Bundle.IsValidItemForThisIngredientDescription`<br>`Data/Bundles 'Pantry/4' ingredient list and slot count (field 4)`<br>`Data/Objects -> Name (duplicate names)` | verified |
| `save.containers` | Pockets are the first <player>/<items>; placed chests and mini-fridges are <Object xsi:type='Chest'> with <items>. Not covered: the farmhouse and island-farmhouse fridge (serialized as a <fridge> element under the FarmHouse/IslandFarmHouse location, no xsi:type), Junimo chests (their contents live in root <globalInventories>, not on the chest), auto-grabbers (contents in <heldObject xsi:type='Chest'>), and farmhands' inventories. Chests in sheds/barns sit inside a building's <indoors> element, so the nearest GameLocation ancestor is the Farm. | `StardewValley.Objects.Chest.GetItemsForPlayer`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.date-fields` | Root SaveGame carries currentSeason (lowercase name), dayOfMonth and year; in 1.6 saves these tags occur only at root, and <money> occurs under <player> first. First-in-document-order works but reading root children directly is safer. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.player-money` | player/<money> is the Farmer._money property: the shared team wallet, or the host's own wallet when separate wallets are on. | `StardewValley.FarmerTeam.GetMoney`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.axe-names` | Data/Tools names the axe tiers Axe (UpgradeLevel 0), Copper Axe 1, Steel Axe 2, Gold Axe 3, Iridium Axe 4. | `Data/Tools -> Name, UpgradeLevel, ClassName` | verified |
| `save.axe-upgradelevel` | Axe items are <Item xsi:type='Axe'> with <upgradeLevel> equal to the Data/Tools UpgradeLevel (0-4). | `Data/Tools -> UpgradeLevel`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.tool-being-upgraded` | When you buy an upgrade at Clint's, the old tool is removed from inventory and toolBeingUpgraded is set to a copy of the NEW tool (e.g. Copper Axe, upgradeLevel 1) with daysLeftForToolUpgrade = 2; it stays there until collected. So owned tier = its upgradeLevel - 1 is correct. | `StardewValley.Tool.actionWhenPurchased`<br>`Data/Tools -> ConventionalUpgradeFrom` | verified |
| `save.rod-tiers` | Data/Tools fishing rods: Bamboo Pole UpgradeLevel 0, Training Rod 1, Fiberglass Rod 2, Iridium Rod 3, Advanced Iridium Rod 4. The planner's own ordinal (Training 0 < Bamboo 1) is a ranking, not the game's upgradeLevel. | `Data/Tools -> Name, UpgradeLevel (ClassName FishingRod)` | verified |
| `save.skill-fields` | Farmer serializes farmingLevel, fishingLevel, foragingLevel, miningLevel, combatLevel (and luckLevel); these are unbuffed base levels. | `StardewValley.Farmer.GetUnmodifiedSkillLevel`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.friendship-fields` | friendshipData is a dictionary keyed by NPC name whose value is a Friendship with Points, GiftsThisWeek, GiftsToday, TalkedToToday, ProposalRejected, Status (Friendly/Dating/Engaged/Married/Divorced), Proposer, RoommateMarriage; hearts = Points / 250. | `StardewValley.Stats.checkForFriendshipAchievements`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.spouse-field` | Farmer.spouse holds the NPC name for both engagement and marriage (also a roommate). The 'engaged' suffix is a pre-1.3 format that SaveMigrator_1_3 strips; since 1.3 engagement is friendshipData[spouse].Status == Engaged. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | to fix |
| `save.mail-received` | mailReceived is a set of <string> mail/flag ids. Letters that have arrived but not yet been read sit in player/<mailbox>, and flags queued for tomorrow in <mailForTomorrow> (normally empty at save time). | `StardewValley.Farmer.hasOrWillReceiveMail`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.events-seen` | eventsSeen is a string set in 1.6 but still serialized with <int> element names; values can be non-numeric (the real save has 'festival_spring13', 'prizeTicketIntro'). | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.fish-caught` | fishCaught: key = qualified fish id '(O)nnn', value = int[2] [times caught, largest size]. Only items whose object type is Fish (or Clam 372) count; trash, fish-pond catches and Algae 167 excluded. The <stats><fishCaught> element is nil in 1.6 and must be skipped (the code does). | `StardewValley.Farmer.caughtFish`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.cooking-recipes` | cookingRecipes is a dictionary: key = known recipe name (Data/CookingRecipes key), value = times cooked. | `Data/CookingRecipes keys` | verified |
| `save.crafting-recipes` | craftingRecipes is a dictionary: key = known recipe name (Data/CraftingRecipes key), value = times crafted. | `Data/CraftingRecipes keys` | verified |
| `save.museum-pieces` | The LibraryMuseum location is named ArchaeologyHouse; museumPieces maps tile (Vector2) -> item id, stored as <string> in 1.6 and <int> in 1.5 saves (confirmed in both). | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.daily-luck` | Root dailyLuck is the team's shared daily luck. The player's effective luck adds +0.025 with the Special Charm and is clamped to [-0.2, 0.2]. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.weather-tomorrow` | Root weatherForTomorrow is the valley (Default context) forecast written after the new day's weather roll: Sun, Rain, Storm, Wind, Snow, GreenRain, Festival or Wedding. Island/Desert have their own in locationWeather. Pre-1.6 saves may hold a legacy int code. | `StardewValley.Game1.ApplyWeatherForNewDay`<br>`StardewValley.Game1.UpdateWeatherForNewDay`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.weather-today-flags` | Root isRaining/isLightning/isSnowing/isDebrisWeather are today's valley weather (the save is written after the day's weather is applied). There is no root isGreenRain: green rain is only in locationWeather['Default'] (isGreenRain, and Weather='GreenRain'), and a green-rain day also sets isRaining true. | `StardewValley.Game1.ApplyWeatherForNewDay`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.weather-precedence` | Storm sets rain+lightning, green rain sets rain, snow excludes rain, so snow > storm > green rain > rain > sun is a consistent order. Wind (isDebrisWeather) is a separate weather the label drops into 'sun'. | `StardewValley.Network.LocationWeather.CopyFrom`<br>`StardewValley.Network.LocationWeather.UpdateDailyWeather`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.buildings` | Every Building has buildingType. In 1.6 the Farmhouse, Greenhouse (present even before it is repaired), Shipping Bin and Pet Bowl are Buildings too. New construction (daysOfConstructionLeft > 0) already has its final type and finishes on its own; an upgrade in progress (daysUntilUpgrade > 0) keeps the OLD buildingType until it finishes (target in upgradeName). | `StardewValley.Buildings.Building.FinishConstruction`<br>`Data/Buildings keys`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.farm-animals` | FarmAnimal.type is the Data/FarmAnimals key (e.g. 'White Chicken', 'Brown Cow'); animals are in each animal building's indoors <animals> dictionary (or the Farm's while outside). | `Data/FarmAnimals keys`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.machines-placed` | A placed big craftable is an <Object> with bigCraftable true in a location's objects. That includes non-machines (chests, scarecrows, rarecrows, signs, statues, Workbench...) and casks. Sprinkler names all end in 'Sprinkler'. Placed Crab Pots are xsi:type CrabPot with bigCraftable false, so they are in neither list. | `Data/Machines keys (qualified (BC) ids) = real machines`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.quest-log` | Quest base fields serialize as id, completed, dailyQuest, daysLeft, moneyReward, _questTitle/questTitle, _currentObjective. Help Wanted subclasses: ItemDeliveryQuest target/item/number (reward is moneyReward), FishingQuest target/whichFish/numberToFish/numberFished/reward, SlayMonsterQuest monsterName/target/numberToKill/numberKilled/reward, ResourceCollectionQuest target/resource/number/numberCollected/reward. The quest type is the xsi:type (absent for plain story quests). | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.special-order-fields` | SpecialOrder serializes questKey, requester, questName and questDescription (tokens like '[Robin_Name]' resolved via Strings/SpecialOrderStrings), orderType ('' town board, 'QiChallenge' Qi), duration, dueDate (absolute WorldDate.TotalDays, 0 = Spring 1 Y1), questState (InProgress/Complete/Failed), preSelectedItems, objectives (xsi:type, currentCount, maxCount, description, acceptableContextTagSets) and rewards. MoneyReward has amount AND multiplier; the payout is amount x multiplier. | `StardewValley.SpecialOrders.Rewards.MoneyReward.GetRewardMoneyAmount`<br>`StardewValley.SpecialOrders.SpecialOrder.SetDuration`<br>`Data/SpecialOrders`<br>`Strings/SpecialOrderStrings`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.special-order-lists` | Root specialOrders = orders the team has accepted (still listed with questState Complete/Failed until removed); availableSpecialOrders = offers on BOTH boards this week (town and Qi's, told apart by orderType); completedSpecialOrders = keys ever completed. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.days-played` | 1.6 keeps days played in the player's stats Values dictionary under key 'daysPlayed' (unsignedInt); older saves had <daysPlayed> under stats (now obsolete_daysPlayed). Spring 1 Y1 = 1. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.professions` | professions is a set of ints; 1 = Tiller, 4 = Artisan. Tiller multiplies sell price by 1.1 for categories -75 (vegetable), -80 (flower) and -79 (fruit) unless the fruit is a spawned forage object; Artisan multiplies -26 artisan goods by 1.4. Multipliers stack per active farmer's professions (shared wallet). | `StardewValley.Object.getPriceAfterMultipliers` | to fix |
| `save.house-upgrade` | houseUpgradeLevel 1 adds the kitchen (FarmHouse1 map has the 'kitchen' tile action). It only increments when Robin finishes (3 days after paying 10,000g + 450 Wood). The Ginger Island farmhouse also has a kitchen. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.ActivateKitchen`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.houseUpgradeAccept`<br>`Maps/FarmHouse1.tmx, Maps/IslandFarmHouse.tmx (Action kitchen)` | to fix |
| `save.deepest-mine` | deepestMineLevel is the max mine level entered (excluding the Quarry Mine 77377); Skull Cavern floor N is stored as 120 + N, so values above 120 are Skull Cavern floors. | `StardewValley.Locations.MineShaft.isSideBranch`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.total-earned` | totalMoneyEarned is farm-wide (team) lifetime earnings; it rises whenever a player's money increases (shipping, sales, quest rewards...), never decreases. | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `save.rusty-key` | In 1.6 the rusty key is the mail flag 'HasRustyKey' on the host (MasterPlayer.mailReceived). The <hasRustyKey> element is obsolete (nil in 1.6 saves; migrated into mail). | `real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |

| `save.saves-location` | Saves are under Environment ApplicationData/StardewValley/Saves: %APPDATA% on Windows; on Linux and macOS .NET resolves ApplicationData to $XDG_CONFIG_HOME, else ~/.config. | `StardewValley.Program.GetAppDataFolder`<br>`StardewValley.Program.GetSavesFolder` | to fix |

| `save.bundle-slot-bools` | Each bundle's save list is 3 entries per ingredient; entry i (0-based, in ingredient order) is true once ingredient i was given. ## Food

| Rule | What the game does | Checked against | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `food.kitchen-needed` | Cooking needs a 'kitchen' tile action, which exists in the upgraded farmhouse/cabin (houseUpgradeLevel >= 1) and in the Ginger Island farmhouse. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.ActivateKitchen`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.houseUpgradeAccept`<br>`Maps/FarmHouse1.tmx, FarmHouse2.tmx, IslandFarmHouse.tmx (Buildings Action 'kitchen')` | to fix |
| `food.qos-learned` | Watching the Queen of Sauce adds the episode's recipe to cookingRecipes immediately if not already known, so the next save has it. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel -> value field 0 (recipe name)` | verified |
| `food.qos-today-only` | The cooking channel airs only on Sundays (new episode: week = (daysPlayed % 224) / 7, with 224 -> 32) and Wednesdays from daysPlayed > 7 (a rerun picked at random from aired episodes some player hasn't learned). Neither can be watched on another day. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.checkForAction`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getRerunWeek`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel` | verified |
| `food.qos-channel-file` | Episodes are Data/TV/CookingChannel (32 entries keyed '1'..'32', value 'Recipe Name/script'); the board text tokens resolve from Strings/SpecialOrderStrings. | `StardewValley.DataLoader.Tv_CookingChannel`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel`<br>`Strings/SpecialOrderStrings` | verified |
| `food.maki-recipe` | Episode 7 is Maki Roll; it airs when daysPlayed = 49, i.e. Summer 21 Y1 (a Sunday), and again every two years (Summer 21 Y3...), plus possible Wednesday reruns. The Saloon (Gus) sells the recipe '(O)228 (Recipe)' for 300g with no condition. | `StardewValley.Objects.TV.getWeeklyRecipe`<br>`Data/Shops Saloon -> (O)228 recipe, Price 300`<br>`Data/TV/CookingChannel key 7` | verified |
| `food.maki-roll` | Maki Roll recipe: 1 of any Fish (category -4) + 1 Seaweed (152) + 1 Rice (423); output 228. Pierre sells Rice. Needs a kitchen. | `StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.ItemMatchesForCrafting`<br>`Data/CookingRecipes 'Maki Roll'`<br>`Data/Shops SeedShop -> (O)423` | verified |
| `food.fried-egg` | Fried Egg recipe: 1 of any Egg-category item (-5: Egg, Large Egg, Duck Egg, Void Egg, Golden Egg, Ostrich Egg); output 194; unlock 'default'. Needs a kitchen. | `StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.ItemMatchesForCrafting`<br>`Data/CookingRecipes 'Fried Egg'`<br>`Data/Objects Category -5` | verified |
| `craft.fish-smoker-jellies` | Fish Smoker (big craftable) costs 10 Hardwood (709) + 1 Sea Jelly + 1 River Jelly + 1 Cave Jelly. | `Data/CraftingRecipes 'Fish Smoker'` | verified |
| `food.energy-formula` | Energy = ceil(Edibility x 2.5) + quality x Edibility for normal items, with two overrides: Stardrop (434) restores 999 energy and Life Elixir (773) restores 0 energy. | `StardewValley.Object.staminaRecoveredOnConsumption`<br>`Data/Objects -> Edibility` | to fix |
| `food.health-formula` | Health = (int)(energy x 0.45) in single-precision (no difference from Python's int(e*0.45) for any vanilla value), except: edibility < 0 gives 0 health, Bug Steak 874 uses 0.68, Stardrop 434 and Energy Tonic 349 give 0, Life Elixir 773 gives 999. | `StardewValley.Object.healthRecoveredOnConsumption`<br>`Data/Objects -> Edibility` | to fix |
| `food.bug-steak-health` | Bug Steak (874) health = (int)(energy x 0.68). | `StardewValley.Object.healthRecoveredOnConsumption` | verified |
| `food.stardrop-tonic-no-health` | Stardrop (434) and Energy Tonic (349) return 0 health. (Separately, Stardrop's energy is 999, not the formula.) | `StardewValley.Object.healthRecoveredOnConsumption`<br>`StardewValley.Object.staminaRecoveredOnConsumption` | verified |
| `food.sell-value` | Sell price = (int)(price x (1 + 0.25 x quality)), then profession multipliers by category, then the farm's profit-margin setting (difficultyModifier) with a floor of 1. Cooked dishes (-7) have no profession bonus, but other edible items do (Tiller fruit/veg/flowers, Artisan -26, Rancher eggs/milk, Fisher/Angler fish, Tapper syrup). | `StardewValley.Object.getPriceAfterMultipliers`<br>`StardewValley.Object.sellToStorePrice`<br>`Data/Objects -> Price, Category` | to fix |
| `food.cooking-needs-kitchen` | Cooking needs a known recipe and a kitchen; kitchens are in the farmhouse/cabin from houseUpgradeLevel 1 and in the Ginger Island farmhouse. The cooking menu also draws ingredients from the kitchen fridge and mini-fridges. | `StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.consumeIngredients`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.ActivateKitchen`<br>`StardewValley.GameLocation.houseUpgradeAccept`<br>`Maps/FarmHouse1.tmx, FarmHouse2.tmx, IslandFarmHouse.tmx (Buildings Action 'kitchen')` | to fix |
| `food.category-ingredients` | A negative ingredient id matches any item whose Category equals it (e.g. -4 any fish, -5 any egg, -6 any milk); quality is ignored. -777 is a special rule for any of the four seasonal wild-seed packs. | `StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.ItemMatchesForCrafting`<br>`StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.isThereSpecialIngredientRule`<br>`Data/CookingRecipes field 0` | verified |
| `food.large-egg-brown-same` | 174 and 182 have identical edibility (15), price (95) and category (-5), so as food they are interchangeable. | `Data/Objects 174, 182` | verified |
| `food.forage-day-window` | Bush berries really are day-windowed (Spring 15-18, Fall 8-11). Location forage is chosen from Data/Locations Forage, filtered by Season and by Condition (e.g. Beach uses Condition 'LOCATION_SEASON Here spring summer fall' with no Season), then rolled against Chance; how many spawn per day is random. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.spawnObjects`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.inBloom`<br>`Data/Locations -> Forage[].Season, Condition, Chance` | to fix |
| `food.max-energy` | Base max stamina is 270; each Stardrop adds 34 (to 508), and MaxStamina buffs add on top. The save stores player <maxStamina>. | `StardewValley.Farmer.doneEating`<br>`real 1.6.15 save (McClarkies) + 1.5.6 save (Morphic)` | verified |
| `food.health-floor-docstring` | For non-negative energy, truncation to int equals floor, so floor(energy x 0.45) is the same as the game's cast (exceptions as in food.health-formula). | `StardewValley.Object.healthRecoveredOnConsumption` | verified |
| `food.edible-rule` | The game lets you eat any object whose Edibility is not -300. Edibility 0 (Daffodil, Sweet Pea, Crocus) and negative values (Sap -1, Holly, Red Mushroom, Pufferfish, Sea Cucumber, Void Egg/Mayonnaise, Goby...) are edible and drain energy. No Arch item has Edibility > 0, so the Arch exclusion does nothing. | `StardewValley.Farmer.eatObject`<br>`Data/Objects -> Edibility, Type` | to fix |
| `food.buff-attrs` | Buffs come from Data/Objects Buffs: Duration is in in-game minutes (-2 = rest of day; omitted with BuffId = Data/Buffs duration); CustomAttributes give the stats. Some foods only reference a BuffId with no CustomAttributes: Beer, Pale Ale, Wine, Mead (17 Tipsy), Oil of Garlic (23), Squid Ink Ravioli (28). Any non-normal quality multiplies the Data/Objects buff duration by 1.5, and cooked dishes (-7) of non-normal quality get +1 on each non-zero stat (farming, fishing, mining, luck, foraging, max energy, magnetism, defense, attack). | `StardewValley.Object.GetFoodOrDrinkBuffs`<br>`StardewValley.Object.ModifyItemBuffs`<br>`StardewValley.Object.TryCreateBuffsFromData`<br>`Data/Buffs`<br>`Data/Objects -> Buffs[].BuffId, Duration, CustomAttributes` | to fix |
| `forage.spring-onion-code` | Each spring day the Forest spawns Spring Onion forage crops in up to 7 random patches in its south-east area (x >= 70, y >= 68 region) on diggable tiles. | `StardewValley.Crop.harvest`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.Forest.DayUpdate` | verified |
| `forage.salmonberry-days` | Medium bushes are in bloom Spring 15-18 (by the bush location's season); each such bush rolls about 20-26% per day to fruit, and shaking a fruiting non-town bush drops Salmonberry (296), 1 + ForagingLevel/4 of them. | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.GetShakeOffItem`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.inBloom`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.loadSprite`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.shake` | verified |
| `forage.blackberry-days` | Same mechanism, Fall 8-11, dropping Blackberry (410). | `StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.GetShakeOffItem`<br>`StardewValley.TerrainFeatures.Bush.inBloom` | verified |
| `forage.winter-root-snow-yam` | Winter Root (412) and Snow Yam (416) come from hoeing ordinary diggable ground in winter: 8% per freshly hoed tile, outdoors, off the Farm, not the Desert, not from bombs. Winter Seeds also grow them. | `StardewValley.GameLocation.checkForBuriedItem`<br>`StardewValley.Tools.Hoe.DoFunction`<br>`Data/Locations -> ArtifactSpots (none for 412/416)` | to fix |
| `forage.cave-carrot` | Cave Carrot (78) comes from Mines barrels/crates, hoeing Mines dirt, and as the fallback of the special mine-level item roll - any season. | `StardewValley.Locations.MineShaft.checkForBuriedItem`<br>`StardewValley.Locations.MineShaft.getSpecialItemForThisMineLevel`<br>`StardewValley.Objects.BreakableContainer.releaseContents` | verified |

| `recipe.unlock-codes` | Cooking field 3 values in data: 'f NPC N' (36): at N hearts the game mails '<NPC>Cooking' next day and reading it teaches the lowest-heart unknown 'f' recipe for that NPC; 's Skill N' (9) or a bare skill name + level: learned on reaching the level (unbuffed); 'default' (1): granted on load; 'l N' (34): informational only (TV/shop recipes, the game ignores it); 'null' (Cookies, from Evelyn's event). | `StardewValley.CraftingRecipe.TryParseLevelRequirement`<br>`StardewValley.Farmer.LearnDefaultRecipes`<br>`StardewValley.Stats.checkForFriendshipAchievements`<br>`Data/CookingRecipes field 3`<br>`Data/CraftingRecipes field 4` | to fix |
